Proves each change per the Phase 3 evidence table (TDD red-green-refactor for behavior changes, suite green + typecheck for mechanical changes) for a given self-contained task. Use for substantial coding work, in parallel when tasks are independent. ([TheElysium/agent-workflow](https://github.com/TheElysium/agent-workflow/blob/HEAD/plugins/agent-workflow/agents/implementer.md))
- **implementer**: Use to build and wire a single delegated unit of work end-to-end in an isolated worktree — implementation, not review. ([TomNeyland/SkillsForThings](https://github.com/TomNeyland/SkillsForThings/blob/HEAD/plugins/conductor/agents/implementer.md) · 2 copies)
- **implementer**: Fills in bodies against a contract the orchestrator already wrote. Give it ONE work package from a ready dossier, in its own isolated worktree. It never sees the tests — they are written concurrently in a different worktree, so its blindness is structural, not a promise. It never changes a signature: a signature the package cannot satisfy comes back as `CONTRACT-CHANGE:` and it stops. It writes source bodies and commits, and nothing else — no metadata, no ADR edits, no branches, no merges, no PR. ([Tristan-Toye/agentic-software-development](https://github.com/Tristan-Toye/agentic-software-development/blob/HEAD/claude/agents/implementer.md))
- **implementer**: Implement the approved plan with the smallest diff, update/add tests, then run verification. ([Trolzie/claude-loop-systems-demo](https://github.com/Trolzie/claude-loop-systems-demo/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/implementer.md))
- **implementer**: Implements ONE scoped issue or brief in its own worktree — tests first, then code, the targeted test and the full gate run and reported truthfully, no push/PR/merge. Use for the implementation stage of the implementation pipeline or any delegated code change. Opus by default; pass model sonnet only when the owner names Sonnet for the run. ([Umut-Demirhan-MBusAn/power_bi_bucket_health](https://github.com/Umut-Demirhan-MBusAn/power_bi_bucket_health/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/implementer.md))
- **implementer**: Code implementer for the Spec-Driven Development workflow. Use when a PLAN.md exists and one phase needs to be executed. Writes code and makes tests pass — nothing else. Applies the relevant engineering-paved-path skills to whichever surface a task touches (backend, frontend, data), and reads the repository's own INSIGHTS.md / architecture docs before writing code. Runs in parallel — spawn one implementer per independent plan phase. ([VadimPoltoratskiy/dev-digest-ai-marketplace](https://github.com/VadimPoltoratskiy/dev-digest-ai-marketplace/blob/HEAD/plugins/sdd-engineering/agents/implementer.md))
- **implementer**: Senior Developer subagent that implements a pre-approved plan with strict TDD (Red/Green/Refactor). ([Valdemird/claude-structure](https://github.com/Valdemird/claude-structure/blob/HEAD/agents/implementer.md))
- **implementer**: Turns the analyst's spec into an idiomatic .NET service over the fixed flat Mongo document model. Writes only under generated/. Also use for post-incident reviews, runbook generation, and monitoring tuning. &lt;example&gt; Context: Production API is returning 500 errors. user: "Our API is throwing 500s in production, users can't log in" assistant: "I'll use the incident-commander… ([nxtg-ai/forge-plugin](https://github.com/nxtg-ai/forge-plugin/blob/HEAD/plugins/nxtg-forge/agents/incident-commander.md) · ★6)
- **incident-commander**: &gt; The calm in the storm -- a crisis response specialist that brings Google SRE structure to chaos, classifies severity in seconds, and ensures every incident leaves the system stronger than before. ([nxtg-ai/forge-plugin](https://github.com/nxtg-ai/forge-plugin/blob/HEAD/docs/agents/incident-commander.md) · ★6)
- **incident-commander**: Use only when incident-ledger.mjs has an open sev1/sev2 record — a production incident, not a routine bug. Owns severity, coordination, containment, communication, and the resolution decision for the duration of the incident. Conditional role, off between incidents. ([vmobifystudio/app-dev-team](https://github.com/vmobifystudio/app-dev-team/blob/HEAD/agents/incident-commander.md) · ★4)
- **incident-commander**: Comandante de incidentes que coordena resposta a problemas em producao. Gerencia comunicacao, escalacao e resolucao de incidentes. Use este agente para: - Coordenar resposta a incidentes - Gerenciar comunicacao durante crises - Documentar timeline de eventos - Escalar para as pessoas certas Examples: - &lt;example&gt; Context: Sistema fora do ar user: "API de pagamentos esta fora do ar" assistant: "Vou usar @incident-commander para coordenar a resposta ao incidente" &lt;commentary&gt; Incidentes precisam de coordenacao centralizada &lt;/commentary&gt; &lt;/example&gt; ([arbgjr/sdlc_agentico](https://github.com/arbgjr/sdlc_agentico/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/incident-commander.md) · ★3 · 4 copies)
- **incident-commander**: Orchestrates the full incident lifecycle for a live Solana protocol (PREPARE→DETECT→TRIAGE→CONTAIN→ERADICATE→RECOVER→LEARN). Use when a protocol is live on mainnet and something is wrong, or to stand up monitoring/preparedness before anything goes wrong. Use when: running an active incident, deriving invariants to watch a program, tracing stolen funds, or producing a post-mortem. ([ansu555/solana-protocol-ops-skill](https://github.com/ansu555/solana-protocol-ops-skill/blob/HEAD/agents/incident-commander.md) · ★2)
- **incident-commander**: Principal Incident Commander with severity / scope / all-clear VETO authority. Loads incident-management skill via reference (PLAN-020 ADR-051). Use for: live incident triage, severity classification, declared-vs-actual scope drift detection, premature all-clear prevention, revocation-latency calls, post-incident review facilitation, on-call rotation design, runbook authoring, paging policy review. Holds VETO on severity assignment, all-clear declaration, and any change affecting detection/paging/recovery surface area during active incidents. ([Canhada-Labs/ceo-orchestration](https://github.com/Canhada-Labs/ceo-orchestration/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/incident-commander.md) · ★2)
- **incident-commander**: Conduz investigação de incidente end-to-end — triagem, preservação de evidência, hipótese, validação e proposta de mitigação. Despachado por /pwdev-devops:incidente. Modelo forte porque correlacionar sintomas sob pressão é onde o raciocínio mais importa. Propõe; nunca executa sozinho.
